DEVELOPMENT ITERATIONS, not the final acceptance run.
These preserve unsuccessful approaches and the subsequent VBV improvement.
Source SHA256 headers are snapshots of disk at run startup. During some initial
compilations tests/comments or the next iteration changed on disk; these hashes
alone are NOT proof of an immutable compiled development snapshot. Final netem
and recovery runs are performed with stable source and checked separately.

Early unit iteration: jitter outlier test failed (12 passed/1 failed); adding
median-of-three before EWMA corrected it. After windowed RTT was introduced,
the historical perpetual-congestion test lost its all-time baseline after 10s.
That test now explicitly supplies periodic unloaded RTT observations while
maintaining backlog, rather than requiring an expired RTT minimum forever.
